   - grad: `h = (p[offsets] @ a)`, `ga = 2*h`, `lam = np.dot(ga, a)`, `grad_a_proj = ga - lam * a`; `grad_x = grad_a_proj[idx_list]`. Return val, grad_x.

   - After run, utilities: `x_to_a(x, n, skip, idx_list)`.

Potential problem: The soft objective val is not the exact score; L-BFGS may drift to reduce soft sum but not exact max. Running with decreasing T mitigates. We accept only if exact improves. If a L-BFGS run worsens exact, we keep old best but final a from that run might be worse; for next lower T, we can start from best exact, not the worsened soft optimum. So for each temp, start from current best. But sometimes a worse soft optimum at higher T can lead to better after lower T. 5. `block_perturb(a, time_limit, rng)`:
   - Similar previous but more sophisticated: random multiplicative exponentials on random intervals, plus single coordinate line search.

     If a_j = α, replacing by α+δ, and renormalizing? If we keep sum1, reduce others? Simpler: optimize a_j while fixing others and renormalizing to sum1? This coupling not exact. Alternatively fix sum by changing a_j and adjusting a reference coordinate? Hard.
     We can use parameterization and L-BFGS for coordinates.
   - Block multiplicative is cheap: candidate = a.copy(); candidate[pos:pos+w] *= exp(noise); normalize; compute objective O(n^2). Good.

Maybe use FFT for h for larger n? Let's revisit Hankel multiplication. We can compute h_j = ∑_{t=0}^{n-1} a_t p_{j+t}. This is convolution of a with a reflected p over a finite range? Let's find correct formula to use FFT and avoid n^2 for n>1000.

We want r_j = ∑_{t=0}^{n-1} a_t p_{j+t}, j=0..n-1.
Define q_m = p_{m} for m=0..2n-2. This is cross-correlation of q and a at lag j: r_j = ∑_{t=0}^{n-1} a_t q_{j+t}.
If we reverse q: q_rev_m = q_{L-1-m}, L=2n-1. Then standard convolution (a * q_rev)_s = ∑_t a_t q_rev_{s-t} = ∑_t a_t q_{L-1-s+t}. We want q_{j+t}. Set L-1-s+t = j+t => s = L-1-j = 2n-2-j. Then (a*q_rev)_{2n-2-j} = ∑_{t} a_t q_{j+t}, but only if q_rev index valid. Great! So h_j = conv(a, q_rev)[2n-2 - j]. Let's test small. n=3, L=5. a=[a0,a1,a2], p length5. q_rev=[p4,p3,p2,p1,p0]. conv length 3+5-1=7. Index s=4-j. So h can be computed by convolution of length n and 2n-1: `cv = np.convolve(a, q_rev, mode='full')` length 3n-2; `h = cv[2*n-2 - np.arange(n)]` (for j=0..n-1, index from 2n-2 down to n-1). Let's more rigorously derive parameter gradient to avoid mistakes.

Parameterization: `z_i = (1-θ_i)y_{s_i} + θ_i y_{s_i+1}` for i=0..n-1. (Assume y_full length K with y0 fixed 0, others variable). `a_i = exp(z_i)/∑exp(z)`. For a softmax over z coordinates, define `G_i = ∂S/∂a_i` (computed). Then for variable y_j (j>0):
∂S/∂y_j = ∑_i G_i ∂a_i/∂y_j.
Let `c_{i,j}` as coefficient of y_j in z_i. For interval s, c_{s}=1-θ, c_{s+1}=θ; others 0.
Let `A_j = ∑_i a_i c_{i,j}` (sum over i of a_i coefficients). Let `C_j = ∑_i G_i a_i c_{i,j}`. Let `D = ∑_i G_i a_i`.

Search structure with time management more concretely:

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + budget_s - 5
    best_a=None; best_score=inf
    # load parent
    parent = get_parent()
    if parent is not None:
       parent_norm=normalize(parent)
       score=exact_score(parent_norm)
       best_a=parent_norm; best_score=score
    n_parent = len(parent) if parent else 300
    # If no parent, best uniform 300.
    if best_a is None: best_a=np.ones(300)/300; best_score=2.0
    # warm phase: local polish of best
    if time left: best_a,best_score = local_polish(best_a, deadline - start - 200, ...)
    # Parametric search on target n maybe best length and high n
    target_ns = [n_parent, max(300,n_parent), 500, 800, 1200] unique <= max_n
    For each n: initialize y from best resized to n if n>best length? For n=300 use parent.
       run parametric search with K values, time.
       After param a, local_polish small.
       Update best.
    # Full L-BFGS on best at its length
    if time: best_a,best_score = full_lbfgs_polish(best_a, time)
    # Block perturb
    if time: block_refine
    return list(best_a)
```
Need ensure functions pass deadline not time? Use time.monotonic inside.

But too many phases could exceed. Use helper `remaining(deadline)`. Each phase checks.

Maybe use a simpler robust approach with time slices:
1. Parent polish (10% budget): subgradient + full LBFGS.
2. Parametric search 300 (20%): K [20,40,80], random + parent.
3. Multi-scale high (20%): upsample best to 1200, param K [60,120], subgain, downsample to 300 and polish.
4. Full LBFGS best (20%).
5. Block/random (10% reserve).

`full_lbfgs_fun`:
```
def full_lbfgs_fun(x, T, n):
    scale = 2.0*n
    a = orig? Need x length n-1. Build a:
    z = np.empty(n); z[0]=0.0; z[1:]=x

`param_soft_fun`:
Input y_params length K-1. Build y_full. z = (1-θ)y[seg]+θ y[seg+1]. Subtract max. exp, a. `val_soft,h,grad=...`. `D=np.dot(grad,a)`? grad is G (sum). For param gradient formula: grad_y_j = C_j - A_j * D_Ga, where D_Ga = ∑ G_i a_i. We can compute `D = np.dot(grad, a)`. Then C=-A? We need G*a.
```
G = grad  # Euclidean gradient of soft max (not multiplied scale)
D = np.dot(G,a)
A=np.zeros(K); C=np.zeros(K)
wa=a; gw=G*a
np.add.at(A, seg, (1-theta)*wa)
np.add.at(A, seg+1, theta*wa)
np.add.at(C, seg, (1-theta)*gw)
np.add.at(C, seg+1, theta*gw)
gY = C - A*D
g_params = scale * gY[1:]
val = scale*val_soft

```
Is D_Ga same as dot(G,a) or dot(grad,a)? yes.

`run_param_lbfgs` implementation:
```
def run_param_lbfgs(n, y0, K, seg, theta, time_limit, temps=(...), maxfun=300):
    best_params = y0[1:].copy(); best_a = param_to_a(...); best_score=exact_score(best_a)
    start=time.monotonic()
    for frac in temps:
       if time elapsed + 10 > time_limit: break
       a_best=param_to_a...
       H=exact_H(a_best)
       T=frac*H
       res = minimize(param_soft_fun, x0=best_params, args=(T,n,K,seg,theta), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={...})
       if res.x finite:
          a_res=param_to_a(...)
          score=exact_score(a_res)
          if score<best_score: best_params=res.x; best_score=score
       # also if res.x score within 1.01, maybe use as x0 for next? Use best always.
    return best_a,best_score
```

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize, nnls

FFT_THRESHOLD = 400  # use FFT for self/h if n > 400? Maybe 600.
OFFSET? not needed for h if use conv formula; for KKT use offsets.

def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    p=1
    while p < m: p <<= 1
    return p

def _fft_convolve(a,b):
    n=len(a); m=len(b); size=n+m-1
    N=_next_pow2(size)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb, N)
    return c[:size]

def conv_self(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n <= 700:
        c=np.convolve(a,a,'full')
    else:
        N=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
        fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
        c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)
        c=c[:2*n-1]
    return np.maximum(c,0.0)

def h_from_p_a(p,a):
    n=len(a)
    q=p[::-1]
    if n <= 700:
        c=np.convolve(a,q,'full')
    else:
        c=_fft_convolve(a,q)
    idx = 2*n-2 - np.arange(n)
    return c[idx]
```
Wait for n=300, q length599, conv length 898. `idx` descending from 598 to 298. Good. For n>700, FFT conv length 3n-2. Need `c` length 3n-2. idx length n. Good.

But `np.convolve(a,q,'full')` for q length 2n-1 length 3n-2. Good.

Could `h_from_p_a` be wrong if p has length 2n-1? yes q reversed. Tested.

s=e.sum()
p=e/s
val=cmax+T*np.log(s)
return val,p
```

def full_lbfgs_fun(x, T, n):
    if n <=1: ...
    z=np.empty(n)
    z[0]=0.0
    z[1:]=x
    maxz = np.max(z)

    e=np.exp(z - z.max())
    s=e.sum()
    if not finite or s<=0: return 1e100, np.zeros(n-1)
    a=e/s
    val_soft, p, h, g = smooth_max_grad(a,T,n)
    scale=2.0*n
    val=scale*val_soft
    D=np.dot(h,a)
    gx=2.0*scale*a[1:]*(h[1:]-D)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)): gx=np.zeros(n-1)
    return val, gx
```

`resize(a,n_new)`:
```
def resize(a,n_new):
    n=len(a)
    if n_new==n: return normalize(a.copy())
    if n_new > n:
        x=np.linspace(0,1,n); y=np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
        out=np.interp(y,x,a)
    else:
        if n % n_new ==0:
            f=n//n_new
            out=a.reshape(n_new,f).sum(axis=1)
        else:
            # weighted resample using cumulative? Simpler interpolation from a to n_new
            x=np.linspace(0,1,n); y=np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
            out=np.interp(y,x,a)
    return normalize(out)
```
For downsample interpolation may not preserve shape mass but normalize.

Maybe final main:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500):

Let's write KKT with active selection and `regularized_solve`:
```
def solve_kkt(M, target, n, rng):
   # NNLS
   try:
      x,res=nnls(M,target)
      cands=[]
      if x.sum()>1e-8: cands.append(x/x.sum())
   except: pass
   # least squares reg
   try:
      lam=1e-6*np.trace(M.T@M)/max(1,n)
      x=np.linalg.solve(M.T@M + lam*np.eye(n), M.T@target)
      x=np.maximum(x,0)
      if x.sum()>1e-8: cands.append(x/x.sum())
   except: pass
   return cands
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500.0):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    reserve = 20.0
    deadline = t_start + total - reserve
    best_a = None; best_score = np.inf
    parent = get_parent()
    if parent is not None and len(parent)>0:
       pa=normalize(parent); pc=exact_score_norm(pa); best_a=pa; best_score=pc
    else:
       n0=300; best_a=np.ones(n0)/n0; best_score=2.0
    n0 = len(best_a)

    # Phase 1: quick local polish parent
    if remaining > 100:
        t=min(120, total*0.1)
        # subgrad
        t_sub = min(40, t*0.3)
        best_a, best_score = subgradient_polish(best_a, t_sub, rng)
        # LP
        if len(best_a)<=LP_MAX_N:
           t_lp=min(40,t*0.4)
           best = lp_ping_pong(best, t_lp, rng)
        # kkt
        if len<=KKT_MAX_N:
           t_kkt=min(20,t*0.2)
           best = kkt_polish(best,t_kkt,rng)
        # full lbfgs short
        t_full=max(0,t-elapsed)
        best=run_full_lbfgs(best,t_full)

    # Phase 2: param search at current n
    n_target = len(best_a)
    if remaining > 100:
        t=min(240, total*0.18)
        best_a, best_score = param_find(n_target, t, best_a, rng, K_values=make K based)

    # Phase 3: multi-scale high
    high_n = min(2000, max(600, n_target*4))
    if remaining > 100 and high_n > n_target:
        t=min(240, total*0.18)
        # param find high from resized best
        a_high, sc_high = param_find(high_n, t*0.7, resize(best_a, high_n), rng, K_values=...)
        if sc_high < best_score: best_a=a_high; best_score=sc_high
        # optimize high sub if time
        if time left: subgradient high
        # downsample to target
        if high_n % n_target ==0:
           a_down=resize(best_a, n_target)
           if score < best: update
           # polish down
           if time left: polish down
    # Phase 4: direct param search for a larger n (e.g. 1000) if not yet
    n_explore = 1000 if best len <=n_explore else int(best len*1.5)
    if remaining >150:
       a_new,sc=param_find(n_explore, t, resize(best_a,n_explore), rng, K_values=[80,160])
       update
    # Phase 5: final polish global best
    if remaining>20:
       t_final=remaining-10
       # if n<=800 full lbfgs, else subgrad/block; kkt if n<=600; lp if n<=600; block
       best=polish_final(best,t_final,rng)
    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```

def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        seq = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if seq.ndim !=1 or seq.size==0: return None
    seq = np.clip(seq,0,1000)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(seq)): return None
    return seq
```

Let's detail `run_param_lbfgs`:
```
def run_param_lbfgs(n, y0, K, seg, theta, time_limit):
    if K <=1: ...
    best_params = np.asarray(y0[1:], dtype=float).copy()
    best_a = param_to_a(best_params, n,K,seg,theta)
    best_score = exact_score(best_a)
    start=time.monotonic()
    # temps depending time
    if time_limit < 5: temps=(0.2,0.05)
    elif time_limit < 20: temps=(0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05)
    else: temps=(0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01)
    eval_est = max(0.002, 1.5e-5*n)
    maxfun = max(50, min(1200, int((time_limit - 2)/max(eval_est*3, 0.005))))
    for frac in temps:
        if time.monotonic()-start > time_limit-2: break
        H = exact_H(best_a)
        T = frac*H if H>0 else 1e-6
        if T < 1e-12: T = 1e-12
        try:
            res = minimize(param_soft_fun, x0=best_params, jac=True, args=(T,n,K,seg,theta), method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxfun':maxfun,'maxiter':maxfun,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-6})
        except Exception:
            continue
        if hasattr(res,'x') and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            a_res = param_to_a(res.x, n,K,seg,theta)
            s_res = exact_score(a_res)
            if s_res < best_score:
                best_score=s_res; best_a=a_res; best_params=res.x.copy()
    return best_a, best_score
```
But `param_soft_fun` needs y_params length K-1; if K=1, y0 length1; not.

`param_soft_fun`:
```
def param_soft_fun(y_params, T, n, K, seg, theta):
    if K <=1: return 1e100, np.empty(0)
    y=np.empty(K); y[0]=0.0; y[1:]=y_params
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)): return 1e100,...
    z = (1.0-theta)*y[seg] + theta*y[seg+1]
    maxz = np.max(z)
    if not np.isfinite(maxz): return ...
    ez = np.exp(z - maxz)
    s = ez.sum()
    if s <=0 or not finite: return ...
    a=ez/s
    val_soft, p, h, g = smooth_max_grad(a,T,n)
    scale=2.0*n
    val=scale*val_soft
    D = float(np.dot(g,a))
    A=np.zeros(K); C=np.zeros(K)
    wa=a
    gw=g*a
    np.add.at(A, seg, (1.0-theta)*wa)
    np.add.at(A, seg+1, theta*wa)
    np.add.at(C, seg, (1.0-theta)*gw)
    np.add.at(C, seg+1, theta*gw)
    gY = C - A*D
    gparams = scale * gY[1:]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gparams)): gparams=np.zeros(K-1)
    return val, gparams
```
Need check `D` using g (Euclidean gradient of soft max). Correct.

But in gradient formula, `A_j = ∑ a_i c_{i,j}`. We computed with a. Good. But if y0 fixed, for j>0 no issue. For j=0, gY not used.

Could there be a scale invariance flat direction in y_params leading to very small gradient? y0 fixed removes additive constant. Good.

`smooth_random_y` implementation careful:
```
def smooth_random_y(K, rng, scale=1.0):
    z = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, K)
    if K > 8:
        Z = np.fft.rfft(z)
        keep = max(2, K//6)
        if keep < len(Z): Z[keep:] = 0.0
        z = np.fft.irfft(Z, n=K)
    if z.std() > 1e-12:
        z = z / z.std()
    slope = rng.uniform(-1.5, 1.5)
    z = z * scale + slope * (np.linspace(-1.0,1.0,K))
    z -= z[0]
    return z
```
Scale maybe 0.5-2.

```
def subgradient_polish(a, time_limit, rng):
    start=time.monotonic()
    best=normalize(a); n=len(best); best_score=exact_score(best)
    step=1.0; frac=0.2; no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic()-start < time_limit:
        H=exact_H(best)
        if H <= 0: break
        T=frac*H
        if T < 1e-12: T=1e-12
        val,p,h,g=smooth_max_grad(best,T,n)
        d = g - np.mean(g)
        d -= d.mean()
        gnorm=np.linalg.norm(d)
        if gnorm < 1e-15:
            frac *=0.5; no_improve +=10; continue
        base_step = 0.05 / gnorm
        if not (0 < base_step < 1e6): base_step=0.1
        if step < base_step: step=base_step? Actually maintain step maybe.
        step = min(1.0, max(step, base_step*0.5))? Simpler: step = base_step initially; after improvements increase.
```

Let's scrutinize gradient in `param_soft_fun` one more time with scale and normalization. The function returns derivative of `S_{scaled}=scale*S_T(a(y))` with respect to y_params. We used `G=grad` from `smooth_max_grad`, which is Euclidean gradient of `S_T(a)` not scaled. Then gY = C - A*D where D=dot(G,a). Multiply scale. Good.

Full L-BFGS gradient: We compute `D=dot(h,a)`, `gx=2*scale*a[1:](h[1:]-D)`. Here `g=2h`, dot(g,a)=2D. ∂S/∂x_j=a_j(g_j - dot(g,a))=2a_j(h_j-D). Multiply scale. Good.

Subgradient Euclidean uses G=2h. d=G-mean(G). This is not the gradient of S_T under normalization? If we constrain sum=1, the Euclidean steepest descent direction is projection of G. Good. But our line search updates a - t d and normalizes (which shouldn't change sum if d sumzero). We clip and renormalize due numeric. Fine.

KKT M uses p and a. For active p, G=2M_p a. KKT wants M_p a constant. We solve `M x = 1`. But if G constant equals 2λ, M x=1. Good.

```python
"""
Step-function coefficient search.

Algorithm summary:
- Work on the normalized simplex (sum of coefficients = 1); the score is scale invariant.
- Use a smooth maximum of the linear convolution as a differentiable surrogate.
- Polish candidates with:
  * projected subgradient steps with exact line search,
  * LP-based alternating-direction moves guaranteed to reduce the peak when successful,
  * L-BFGS-B in log-ratio coordinates on the full vector,
  * L-BFGS-B in low-dimensional log-spline coordinates,
  * KKT-inspired active-set linear / multiplicative moves,
  * multistart and multi-scale (up/down-sampling) re-optimization.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize, nnls

CONV_NP_LIMIT = 512
LP_MAX_N = 600
KKT_MAX_N = 600
FULL_LBFGS_MAX_N = 1200


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def fft_convolve(a, b):
    size = len(a) + len(b) - 1
    N = next_pow2(size)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, N)
    return c[:size]


def conv_self(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if n <= CONV_NP_LIMIT:
        c = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    else:
        N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
        c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def h_from_p_a(p, a):
    # h_j = sum_t a_t p_{j+t}, j=0..n-1.
    # Use identity: h_j = convolve(a, reverse(p))_{2n-2-j}.
    n = len(a)
    q = p[::-1]
    if n <= CONV_NP_LIMIT:
        c = np.convolve(a, q, mode="full")
    else:
        c = fft_convolve(a, q)
    idx = 2 * n - 2 - np.arange(n)
    h = c[idx]
    return np.maximum(h, 0.0)


def soft_max_probs(c, T):
    if T <= 1e-14:
        T = 1e-14
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    shifted = (c - cmax) / T
    shifted = np.clip(shifted, -500.0, 500.0)
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.full_like(c, 1.0 / max(1, c.size))
        return cmax, p
    p = e / s
    val = cmax + T * float(np.log(s))
    return val, p


def smooth_max_grad(a, T, n):
    if T <= 1e-14:
        T = 1e-14
    c = conv_self(a)
    val, p = soft_max_probs(c, T)
    h = h_from_p_a(p, a)
    grad = 2.0 * h
    return val, p, h, grad


def normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-30 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones_like(a, dtype=float) / a.size
    return a / s


def exact_H(a):
    c = conv_self(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.max(c))


def exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    H = exact_H(a)
    return float(2.0 * a.size * H / (s * s))


def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        seq = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if seq.ndim != 1 or seq.size == 0:
        return None
    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(seq)):
        seq = np.nan_to_num(seq, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    return seq


def resize(a, n_new):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0 or n_new <= 0:
        return a
    if n == n_new:
        return normalize(a.copy())

    if n_new > n:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
        out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    else:
        if n % n_new == 0:
            f = n // n_new
            out = a.reshape(n_new, f).sum(axis=1)
        else:
            x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
            out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize(out)


def make_param_grid(n, K):
    K = max(2, min(K, n))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    u = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, K)
    j = np.floor(t * (K - 1)).astype(int)
    j = np.clip(j, 0, K - 2)
    denom = u[j + 1] - u[j]
    denom = np.where(denom == 0.0, 1.0, denom)
    theta = (t - u[j]) / denom
    theta = np.clip(theta, 0.0, 1.0)
    return K, j, theta


def param_to_a(y_params, n, K, seg, theta):
    if K <= 1:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    y_params = np.asarray(y_params, dtype=float)
    if y_params.size != K - 1:
        y_params = y_params[:K - 1]
        if y_params.size < K - 1:
            y_params = np.pad(y_params, (0, K - 1 - y_params.size), constant_values=0.0)
    y = np.empty(K, dtype=float)
    y[0] = 0.0
    y[1:] = y_params
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)

    z = (1.0 - theta) * y[seg] + theta * y[seg + 1]
    maxz = float(np.max(z)) if z.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(maxz):
        maxz = 0.0
    ez = np.exp(z - maxz)
    s = float(ez.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    return ez / s


def y_from_a(a, K):
    K = max(2, int(K))
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(K)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    u = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, K)
    vals = np.interp(u, x, a)
    vals = np.maximum(vals, 1e-15)
    y = np.log(vals)
    y -= y[0]
    return y


def smooth_random_y(K, rng, scale=1.0):
    K = max(2, int(K))
    z = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, K)
    if K > 8:
        Z = np.fft.rfft(z)
        keep = max(2, K // 6)
        if keep < len(Z):
            Z[keep:] = 0.0
        z = np.fft.irfft(Z, n=K)
    sd = float(z.std())
    if sd > 1e-12:
        z = z / sd
    slope = rng.uniform(-1.5, 1.5)
    z = z * scale + slope * np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, K)
    z -= z[0]
    return z


def make_random_a(n, rng):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    y = smooth_random_y(n, rng, scale=1.0)
    a = np.exp(y - np.max(y))
    return normalize(a)


def full_lbfgs_fun(x, T, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if n <= 1:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(0)
    if x.size != n - 1 or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1)

    z = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    z[0] = 0.0
    z[1:] = x
    maxz = float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(maxz):
        maxz = 0.0
    ez = np.exp(z - maxz)
    s = float(ez.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1)
    a = ez / s

    val_soft, p, h, g = smooth_max_grad(a, T, n)
    scale = 2.0 * n
    val = scale * val_soft
    D = float(np.dot(h, a))
    gx = 2.0 * scale * a[1:] * (h[1:] - D)
    gx = np.where(np.isfinite(gx), gx, 0.0)
    return float(val), gx


def run_full_lbfgs(a, time_limit):
    a = normalize(a)
    n = len(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = exact_score(best_a)
    if n <= 1 or time_limit <= 3.0:
        return best_a, best_score

    start = time.monotonic()
    eps = max(float(np.max(a)) * 1e-12, 1e-15)
    x0 = np.log((a[1:] + eps) / (a[0] + eps))
    x0 = np.where(np.isfinite(x0), x0, 0.0)
    current_params = x0.copy()

    eval_est = max(0.002, 1.0e-5 * n)
    maxfun = int(200 + 0.6 * (time_limit / max(eval_est * 3.0, 0.003)))
    maxfun = max(200, min(2500, maxfun))
    if n > 800:
        maxfun = min(maxfun, 700)
    if n > FULL_LBFGS_MAX_N:
        return best_a, best_score

    if time_limit < 10.0:
        temps = (0.2, 0.05)
    elif time_limit < 40.0:
        temps = (0.5, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05)
    else:
        temps = (0.5, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005)

    for frac in temps:
        if time.monotonic() - start > time_limit - 2.0:
            break
        H = exact_H(best_a)
        T = frac * H if H > 0.0 else 1e-6
        T = max(1e-12, min(T, 1e-2))
        try:
            res = minimize(
                full_lbfgs_fun,
                x0=current_params,
                args=(T, n),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options={
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "maxiter": maxfun,
                    "maxls": 20,
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-6,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            continue
        if not hasattr(res, "x"):
            continue
        res_x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
        if res_x.size != n - 1 or not np.all(np.isfinite(res_x)):
            continue

        # Convert back to a by adding reference coordinate 0.
        z = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
        z[0] = 0.0
        z[1:] = res_x
        maxz = float(np.max(z))
        ez = np.exp(z - maxz)
        a_res = ez / ez.sum()
        a_res = normalize(a_res)
        sc = exact_score(a_res)

        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a_res
            current_params = res_x.copy()
        elif sc < best_score * 1.02:
            current_params = res_x.copy()

    return best_a, best_score


def param_soft_fun(y_params, T, n, K, seg, theta):
    y_params = np.asarray(y_params, dtype=float)
    if K <= 1:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(0)
    if y_params.size != K - 1 or not np.all(np.isfinite(y_params)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(max(0, K - 1))

    y = np.empty(K, dtype=float)
    y[0] = 0.0
    y[1:] = y_params
    z = (1.0 - theta) * y[seg] + theta * y[seg + 1]
    maxz = float(np.max(z)) if z.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(maxz):
        maxz = 0.0
    ez = np.exp(z - maxz)
    s = float(ez.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(K - 1)
    a = ez / s

    val_soft, p, h, g = smooth_max_grad(a, T, n)
    scale = 2.0 * n
    val = scale * val_soft

    # Gradient through linear interpolation of log-weights.
    D = float(np.dot(g, a))
    A = np.zeros(K, dtype=float)
    C = np.zeros(K, dtype=float)
    one_minus = 1.0 - theta
    np.add.at(A, seg, one_minus * a)
    np.add.at(A, seg + 1, theta * a)
    gw = g * a
    np.add.at(C, seg, one_minus * gw)
    np.add.at(C, seg + 1, theta * gw)
    gY = C - A * D
    gparams = scale * gY[1:]
    gparams = np.where(np.isfinite(gparams), gparams, 0.0)
    return float(val), gparams


def run_param_lbfgs(n, y0, K, seg, theta, time_limit):
    K, seg, theta = make_param_grid(n, K)
    y0 = np.asarray(y0, dtype=float)
    if y0.size < K:
        y0 = np.pad(y0, (0, K - y0.size), constant_values=0.0)
    best_params = y0[1:].copy()
    best_a = param_to_a(best_params, n, K, seg, theta)
    best_score = exact_score(best_a)
    current_params = best_params.copy()

    if time_limit <= 2.0:
        return best_a, best_score

    start = time.monotonic()
    eval_est = max(0.002, 1.6e-5 * n)
    maxfun = int(50 + 0.7 * (time_limit / max(eval_est * 3.0, 0.004)))
    maxfun = max(50, min(1500, maxfun))

    if time_limit < 8.0:
        temps = (0.2, 0.05)
    elif time_limit < 25.0:
        temps = (0.5, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05)
    else:
        temps = (0.5, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01)

    for frac in temps:
        if time.monotonic() - start > time_limit - 1.5:
            break
        H = exact_H(best_a)
        T = frac * H if H > 0.0 else 1e-6
        T = max(1e-12, min(T, 1e-2))
        try:
            res = minimize(
                param_soft_fun,
                x0=current_params,
                args=(T, n, K, seg, theta),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options={
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "maxiter": maxfun,
                    "maxls": 20,
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-6,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            continue
        if not hasattr(res, "x"):
            continue
        rx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
        if rx.size != K - 1 or not np.all(np.isfinite(rx)):
            continue
        a_res = param_to_a(rx, n, K, seg, theta)
        sc = exact_score(a_res)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a_res
            best_params = rx.copy()
            current_params = rx.copy()
        elif sc < best_score * 1.02:
            current_params = rx.copy()

    return best_a, best_score


def param_find(n, time_limit, initial_a, rng):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    if time_limit <= 5.0:
        if initial_a is not None:
            a = resize(initial_a, n)
        else:
            a = make_random_a(n, rng)
        return a, exact_score(a)

    if initial_a is not None:
        best = resize(initial_a, n)
    else:
        best = make_random_a(n, rng)
    best_score = exact_score(best)

    K_list = [
        max(8, min(30, n // 12)),
        max(12, min(60, n // 8)),
        max(20, min(120, n // 6)),
        max(30, min(200, n // 4)),
    ]
    K_list = sorted(set([max(2, min(k, n)) for k in K_list]))[:4]

    start = time.monotonic()
    nK = len(K_list)

    for Ki, K in enumerate(K_list):
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        remaining = time_limit - elapsed
        if remaining < 5.0:
            break
        time_per_K = remaining / max(1, nK - Ki)
        K, seg, theta = make_param_grid(n, K)

        inits = []
        inits.append(y_from_a(best, K))

        if time_per_K > 35.0:
            nrand = 3
        elif time_per_K > 12.0:
            nrand = 2
        else:
            nrand = 1
        for ri in range(nrand):
            inits.append(smooth_random_y(K, rng, scale=0.6 + 0.35 * ri))

        time_per_init = max(3.0, time_per_K / max(1, len(inits)))
        for y0 in inits:
            if time.monotonic() - start > time_limit - 3.0:
                break
            a_res, sc_res = run_param_lbfgs(n, y0, K, seg, theta, time_per_init)
            if sc_res < best_score:
                best_score = sc_res
                best = a_res
                qtime = min(5.0, max(0.0, time_limit - (time.monotonic() - start)) * 0.25)
                if qtime > 2.0:
                    a_q, sc_q = subgradient_polish(best, qtime, rng)
                    if sc_q < best_score:
                        best_score = sc_q
                        best = a_q

    return best, best_score


def subgradient_polish(a, time_limit, rng):
    if time_limit <= 1.0:
        a = normalize(a)
        return a, exact_score(a)
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(0)

    best_a = normalize(a)
    best_score = exact_score(best_a)
    ever_a = best_a.copy()
    ever_score = best_score

    start = time.monotonic()
    n = best_a.size
    step_mult = 1.0
    frac = 0.2
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() - start < time_limit:
        if n <= 1:
            break
        H = exact_H(best_a)
        if H <= 0.0:
            break
        T = frac * H
        T = max(1e-12, min(T, 1e-2))

        val, p, h, g = smooth_max_grad(best_a, T, n)
        d = g - float(np.mean(g))
        d -= float(d.mean())
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if gnorm < 1e-15:
            frac *= 0.5
            no_improve += 5
            if frac < 1e-5:
                break
            continue

        step = 0.05 * step_mult / gnorm
        if not np.isfinite(step) or step <= 0.0:
            step = 1e-12
        step = min(step, 1.0)

        neg = d < 0.0
        tmax = 1.0
        if np.any(neg):
            ratios = best_a[neg] / (-d[neg])
            if ratios.size:
                tmax = float(np.min(ratios)) * 0.95
            else:
                tmax = 0.0
        if tmax <= 1e-14:
            break
        t = min(step, tmax)

        found = False
        for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05):
            tt = min(t * factor, tmax)
            if tt <= 0.0:
                continue
            cand = best_a - tt * d
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            s = float(cand.sum())
            if s < 1e-12:
                continue
            cand = cand / s
            sc = exact_score(cand)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_a = cand
                best_score = sc
                if sc < ever_score:
                    ever_score = sc
                    ever_a = cand.copy()
                step_mult = min(3.0, step_mult * 1.2)
                no_improve = 0
                found = True
                break

        if found:
            continue

        no_improve += 1
        step_mult *= 0.6

        if no_improve > 80:
            frac *= 0.75
            if frac < 1e-5:
                break
        if no_improve > 180:
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)
            cand = best_a * (1.0 + noise)
            cand = normalize(cand)
            sc = exact_score(cand)
            if sc < best_score * 1.05:
                best_a = cand
                best_score = sc
                if sc < ever_score:
                    ever_score = sc
                    ever_a = cand.copy()
                step_mult = 1.0
                frac = max(1e-4, frac)
                no_improve = 0
                continue
            break

        if step_mult < 1e-4:
            step_mult = 1.0

    if best_score < ever_score:
        ever_a = best_a.copy()
        ever_score = best_score
    return normalize(ever_a), float(ever_score)


def build_A(a):
    n = len(a)
    rows = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.zeros((rows, n), dtype=float)
    for k in range(rows):
        start = max(0, k - n + 1)
        end = min(n - 1, k)
        if start <= end:
            A[k, start:end + 1] = a[k - end:k - start + 1][::-1]
    return A


def lp_ping_pong(a, time_limit, rng):
    if time_limit <= 1.0:
        a = normalize(a)
        return a, exact_score(a)
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(1)

    best_a = normalize(a)
    best_score = exact_score(best_a)
    n = best_a.size
    if n <= 1 or n > LP_MAX_N:
        return best_a, best_score

    rows = 2 * n - 1
    ones = np.ones(n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 50.0)] * n
    start = time.monotonic()
    no_improve = 0
    iters = 0

    while time.monotonic() - start < time_limit - 1.0 and iters < 5000:
        iters += 1
        H = exact_H(best_a)
        if H <= 0.0:
            break
        try:
            A = build_A(best_a)
            res = linprog(
                -ones,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=np.full(rows, H),
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True},
            )
        except Exception:
            break
        if not res.success or res.x is None:
            break

        b = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
        b = np.clip(b, 0.0, None)
        bs = float(b.sum())
        if bs <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(bs):
            break
        b = b / bs

        found = False
        t_list = (0.5, 0.25, 0.75, 0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.8, 0.05, 0.9)
        for t in t_list:
            if t <= 0.0 or t >= 1.0:
                continue
            cand = (1.0 - t) * best_a + t * b
            cand = normalize(cand)
            sc = exact_score(cand)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_a = cand
                best_score = sc
                found = True
                break

        if found:
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        no_improve += 1
        if no_improve > 2:
            H2 = H * 0.9
            try:
                A2 = build_A(best_a)
                res2 = linprog(
                    -ones,
                    A_ub=A2,
                    b_ub=np.full(rows, H2),
                    bounds=bounds,
                    method="highs",
                    options={"presolve": True},
                )
            except Exception:
                res2 = None
            if res2 is not None and res2.success and res2.x is not None:
                b2 = np.asarray(res2.x, dtype=float)
                b2 = np.clip(b2, 0.0, None)
                bs2 = float(b2.sum())
                if bs2 > 1e-12 and np.isfinite(bs2):
                    b2 = b2 / bs2
                    for t in (0.1, 0.2, 0.05, 0.3):
                        cand = (1.0 - t) * best_a + t * b2
                        cand = normalize(cand)
                        sc = exact_score(cand)
                        if sc < best_score:
                            best_a = cand
                            best_score = sc
                            found = True
                            break
            no_improve = 0

        if no_improve > 8:
            break
        if no_improve > 5:
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.01, n)
            cand = normalize(best_a * (1.0 + noise))
            sc = exact_score(cand)
            if sc < best_score * 1.03:
                best_a = cand
                best_score = sc
                no_improve = 0

    return best_a, best_score


def kkt_polish(a, time_limit):
    if time_limit <= 1.0:
        a = normalize(a)
        return a, exact_score(a)

    best_a = normalize(a)
    best_score = exact_score(best_a)
    n = best_a.size
    if n <= 2 or n > KKT_MAX_N:
        return best_a, best_score

    idx = np.arange(n)
    offsets = idx[:, None] + idx[None, :]
    target = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    eye = np.eye(n)
    start = time.monotonic()

    p_modes = []
    p_modes.append(("soft01", 0.1))
    p_modes.append(("soft005", 0.05))
    p_modes.append(("active", 0.02))

    for mode, alpha in p_modes:
        if time.monotonic() - start > time_limit - 1.0:
            break

        H = exact_H(best_a)
        if H <= 0.0:
            continue
        c = conv_self(best_a)
        if mode == "active":
            mask = c >= H - alpha * H
            if count = int(mask.sum()); count > 0:
                p = np.zeros_like(c)
                p[mask] = 1.0 / count
            else:
                continue
        else:
            val, p = soft_max_probs(c, alpha * H)

        M = p[offsets]
        candidates = []

        try:
            x_nnls, _ = nnls(M, target)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(x_nnls)) and float(x_nnls.sum()) > 1e-10:
                candidates.append(x_nnls / x_nnls.sum())
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            MTM = M.T @ M
            lam = 1e-6 * (float(np.trace(MTM)) / max(1, n))
            x_reg = np.linalg.solve(MTM + lam * eye, M.T @ target)
            x_reg = np.maximum(x_reg, 0.0)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(x_reg)) and float(x_reg.sum()) > 1e-10:
                candidates.append(x_reg / x_reg.sum())
        except Exception:
            pass

        for x in candidates:
            for eta in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05):
                cand = eta * x + (1.0 - eta) * best_a
                cand = normalize(cand)
                sc = exact_score(cand)
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_a = cand
                    best_score = sc

        # Multiplicative constant-h iteration.
        if time.monotonic() - start < time_limit - 1.0:
            h = M.dot(best_a)
            eps = np.finfo(float).eps
            ratio = best_a / (h + eps)
            for beta in (0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0):
                cand = normalize(best_a * np.power(np.maximum(ratio, 0.0), beta))
                sc = exact_score(cand)
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_a = cand
                    best_score = sc

    return best_a, best_score


def block_refine(a, time_limit, rng):
    if time_limit <= 1.0:
        a = normalize(a)
        return a, exact_score(a)
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(2)

    best_a = normalize(a)
    best_score = exact_score(best_a)
    n = best_a.size
    if n <= 2:
        return best_a, best_score

    start = time.monotonic()
    iters = 0
    while time.monotonic() - start < time_limit - 0.5:
        iters += 1
        width = max(1, int(n * rng.uniform(0.02, 0.15)))
        if width >= n:
            pos = 0
        else:
            pos = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))

        base = best_a.copy()
        seg = base[pos:pos + width].copy()
        best_factor_candidate = None
        best_sc = best_score

        factors = [0.5, 0.7, 0.9, 1.1, 1.3, 1.5]
        factors.append(float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.15))))
        factors = sorted([max(0.01, f) for f in factors])

        for f in factors:
            cand = base.copy()
            cand[pos:pos + width] = seg * f
            cand = normalize(cand)
            sc = exact_score(cand)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_factor_candidate = cand

        if best_factor_candidate is not None:
            best_a = best_factor_candidate
            best_score = best_sc

        if iters % 2000 == 0:
            print(f"  block_refine iter={iters} score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)

    return best_a, best_score


def polish_final(a, time_limit, rng):
    best_a = normalize(a)
    best_score = exact_score(best_a)
    n = best_a.size

    if time_limit <= 5.0:
        return best_a, best_score

    start = time.monotonic()

    def remain():
        return max(0.0, time_limit - (time.monotonic() - start))

    if n <= LP_MAX_N and remain() > 10.0:
        t = min(30.0, remain() * 0.20)
        best_a, best_score = lp_ping_pong(best_a, t, rng)

    if n <= KKT_MAX_N and remain() > 8.0:
        t = min(25.0, remain() * 0.12)
        best_a, best_score = kkt_polish(best_a, t)

    if remain() > 15.0:
        t = min(55.0, remain() * 0.25)
        best_a, best_score = subgradient_polish(best_a, t, rng)

    if n <= FULL_LBFGS_MAX_N and remain() > 25.0:
        t = min(80.0, remain() * 0.35)
        best_a, best_score = run_full_lbfgs(best_a, t)

    if remain() > 10.0:
        t = remain() - 2.0
        best_a, best_score = block_refine(best_a, t, rng)

    return best_a, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500.0):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    reserve = 20.0
    deadline = t_start + total - reserve

    def time_left():
        return max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())

    best_a = None
    best_score = np.inf

    parent = get_parent_sequence()
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        pa = normalize(parent)
        pc = exact_score(pa)
        best_a = pa
        best_score = pc
        print(f"Parent length={pa.size}, score={pc:.7f}", flush=True)
    else:
        n0 = 300
        best_a = np.full(n0, 1.0 / n0)
        best_score = exact_score(best_a)
        print(f"No parent; using uniform length={n0}, score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)

    n0 = int(best_a.size)

    # 1. Quick local polish of the starting construction.
    if time_left() > 120.0:
        t = min(150.0, total * 0.12)
        print(f"Phase 1: local polish, budget={t:.1f}s", flush=True)
        t_sub = min(40.0, t * 0.35)
        best_a, best_score = subgradient_polish(best_a, t_sub, rng)
        if len(best_a) <= LP_MAX_N and time_left() > 40.0:
            t_lp = min(45.0, t * 0.30)
            best_a, best_score = lp_ping_pong(best_a, t_lp, rng)
        if len(best_a) <= KKT_MAX_N and time_left() > 25.0:
            t_kkt = min(25.0, t * 0.20)
            best_a, best_score = kkt_polish(best_a, t_kkt)
        if len(best_a) <= FULL_LBFGS_MAX_N and time_left() > 30.0:
            t_f = max(0.0, t - (t_sub + 0.30 * t + 0.20 * t))
            if t_f > 10.0:
                best_a, best_score = run_full_lbfgs(best_a, t_f)
        print(f"  after phase 1: n={best_a.size} score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)

    # 2. Parametric search at the natural length.
    if time_left() > 120.0:
        n_target = int(best_a.size)
        t = min(240.0, total * 0.18)
        print(f"Phase 2: parametric search n={n_target}, budget={t:.1f}s", flush=True)
        a2, sc2 = param_find(n_target, t, best_a, rng)
        if sc2 < best_score:
            best_a, best_score = a2, sc2
            print(f"  after phase 2: n={best_a.size} score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)
        else:
            print(f"  after phase 2 (best unchanged): score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)

    # 3. Multi-scale: upscale, optimize, downsample.
    if time_left() > 120.0:
        high_n = int(min(1600, max(600, max(2, best_a.size) * 4)))
        if high_n > best_a.size:
            t = min(240.0, total * 0.18)
            print(f"Phase 3: multi-scale up to n={high_n}, budget={t:.1f}s", flush=True)
            a_high, sc_high = param_find(high_n, t * 0.70, best_a, rng)
            if time_left() > 15.0:
                t_sub = max(5.0, t * 0.15)
                a_high, sc_high = subgradient_polish(a_high, t_sub, rng)
            if sc_high < best_score:
                best_a, best_score = a_high, sc_high
                print(f"  high-n improved: n={best_a.size} score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)

            # Downsample to the original/target length and polish.
            low_n = int(best_a.size if best_a.size != high_n else max(2, high_n // 4))
            a_down = resize(a_high, low_n)
            sc_down = exact_score(a_down)
            if sc_down < best_score:
                best_a, best_score = a_down, sc_down
                print(f"  downsample improved: n={best_a.size} score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)
            if time_left() > 50.0:
                best_a, best_score = polish_final(best_a, min(90.0, time_left() * 0.40), rng)
                print(f"  after phase 3: n={best_a.size} score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)
            else:
                print(f"  after phase 3: score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)
        else:
            print("Phase 3 skipped: no useful high resolution.", flush=True)

    # 4. Explore a larger / different length.
    explore_n = int(min(1200, max(600, max(2, best_a.size) + 400)))
    if time_left() > 120.0 and explore_n != int(best_a.size):
        t = min(180.0, total * 0.14)
        print(f"Phase 4: explore n={explore_n}, budget={t:.1f}s", flush=True)
        a4, sc4 = param_find(explore_n, t, best_a, rng)
        if sc4 < best_score:
            best_a, best_score = a4, sc4
            print(f"  after phase 4: n={best_a.size} score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)
        else:
            print(f"  after phase 4 (best unchanged): score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)

    # 5. Final global polish.
    if time_left() > 20.0:
        t = time_left() - 8.0
        print(f"Phase 5: final polish, budget={t:.1f}s", flush=True)
        best_a, best_score = polish_final(best_a, t, rng)
        print(f"  after final polish: n={best_a.size} score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)

    best_a = normalize(np.asarray(best_a, dtype=float))
    if best_a.size == 0:
        best_a = np.full(300, 1.0 / 300.0)
    out = [max(0.0, float(x)) for x in best_a]

    try:
        eval_score = evaluate_sequence(out)
        print(f"Final returned score via evaluate_sequence: {eval_score:.7f}", flush=True)
    except Exception:
        pass
    print(f"Done. n={len(out)} internal_score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)
    return out
```
